West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Open with take egg (+5), window west (+10), put egg in case (+5), then trap door 'down' (+25). All were recorded; repeat without detours.
2. Light the lantern before descending. Push and open room features (rug, trap door, case) when a scene hints at them; this exposed the +25 route.
3. The trap door shut behind the Actor and no return route was found. Bank treasures in the case before going down.
4. Gallery 'take art' scored +4 once. Take valuables once and keep them; do not drop and re-take them to 'test' scoring.
5. When stalled, pick an exit never used (Studio's other exits, Attic, new Cellar options) rather than a known room; new rooms are the only source of fresh points.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Do not re-enter Troll Room from Cellar. About eight visits scored nothing, and the last 'north' knocked the Actor out and ended the game at -10.
2. Do not cycle Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Troll Room. About 150 steps there scored 0; after two repeats of an edge, change goal.
3. Do not follow the 'take coins' Maze goal when the route is blocked; it was reissued about ten times and never became reachable.
4. Do not repeat put down/take on the same item (leaflet five times, art once). Each pair wasted steps and changed nothing.
5. Never pick up the rusty knife or hit things with it (-10 earlier). Avoid Maze ping-pong rooms 67-70 and 167.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

Historian episode review — episode 44
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

The Actor opened well: the recorded events show take egg +5, west into the Kitchen +10, put egg in case +5 and trap door down +25. It also took the Gallery art for +4. Navigation was clean, with no deviations and every transition confirmed, so the failures were the Actor's choices. After the coins (+10 in room 167) it spent roughly the last 85 steps looping among Maze rooms 67, 68, 69, 70, 167, 65 and 66 with no score and no new rooms. Its alternating explore/investigate goals never produced a new exit. It then took the rusty knife, took the skeleton (a ghost cursed it), and hit the thief with the sword. Taking the sword back ended the game at -10. Note that the briefing places the death in the Troll Room and the final score at 39, while the episode log shows a Maze death and 55. The log also records the painting deposit twice (+6 each), which I cannot fully reconcile with the briefing. Next episode: leave the Maze, test Studio 'up' and other untried exits, and stop any edge once it has repeated twice.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Open with take egg (+5), west into Kitchen (+10), put egg in case (+5), trap door down (+25). All four were recorded this episode.
2. From Studio, test 'up' and every other untried exit first. Confirm painting banking by a score event and inventory, not by assumption.
3. Take Gallery art once (+4) and keep it. Deposit it in the case only after a verified route back to the Living Room.
4. Prefer unvisited rooms and unverified exits. Once an edge repeats twice with no score, switch goal and leave that room cluster.
5. Light the lantern before going down. Examine room features when the text hints at them, and check score and inventory after each action.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Never go north from Cellar into the Troll Room. The troll kills, and earlier Troll Room visits scored nothing.
2. Do not cycle Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio. About 130 steps there scored 0; treat any three-time repeated move as a loop.
3. Do not ping-pong Maze rooms 63-70, 167, 65 and 66. About 85 steps after the coins gave no score and no new rooms.
4. Do not take the rusty knife or skeleton, or attack the thief. The result was a ghost curse, a lost sword and death at -10.
5. Do not set the Maze 'take coins' goal, and do not repeat drop/take pairs on the same item. Change approach after one failure.
